    '  );\r\n' +

    '  const itemsRef = useRef(items);\r\n' +
    '  useEffect(() => {\r\n' +
    '    itemsRef.current = items;\r\n' +
    '  }, [items]);\r\n' +
    '  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!initialItems);\r\n' +
    '  const [loadingMore, setLoadingMore] = useState(false);\r\n' +
    '  const [summaryLoading, setSummaryLoading] = useState(true);\r\n' +
    "  const [error, setError] = useState('');\r\n" +
    '  const [nextCursor, setNextCursor] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n' +
    '  const [revision, setRevision] = useState(0);\r\n' +
    '  const generation = useRef(0);\r\n' +
    '  const moreController = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);\r\n' +
    '  const currentDay = useRef(day);\r\n' +
    '  const previousQuery = useRef(query);\r\n' +
    '  const loadedDay = useRef(day);\r\n' +
    '  const loadedQuery = useRef<string | null>(null);\r\n' +
    '  const failedMore = useRef(false);\r\n' +
    "  const savedSummaries = useRef(new Map<string, SavedParameterReading['summary']>());\r\n" +
    '  useEffect(() => {\r\n' +
    '    currentDay.current = day;\r\n' +
    '  }, [day]);\r\n' +

    '    () => ({\r\n' +
    '      q: query.trim() || undefined,\r\n' +

    '    },\r\n' +

    '        setItems((current) => current.map((item) => resetParameterDay(item, day)));\r\n' +
    '      }\r\n' +
    '      // Identities and daily metadata are independent. The detailed page remains\r\n' +
    '      // authoritative for membership/cursor, including room searches.\r\n' +

    '          { limit: 25, ...rosterOptions, asOf: day },\r\n' +

    '          },\r\n' +
    '        )\r\n' +
    '          .then((page) => {\r\n' +

    '            setItems(\r\n' +

    '                patient,\r\n' +
    '                cartella: { pazienteId: patient.id, parametriMensili: [] },\r\n' +
    '                summaryPending: true,\r\n' +

    '            );\r\n' +
    '            setLoading(false);\r\n' +

    '          .catch(() => {\r\n' +
    '            /* The parameter page handles failure and can still supply identities. */\r\n' +
    '          });\r\n' +
    '      }\r\n' +
    '      void (async () => {\r\n' +
    "        const filterKey = JSON.stringify([filters.q ?? '', rosterKey]);\r\n" +

    '          setNextCursor(page.nextCursor);\r\n' +
    '          cursor = needsMore ? page.nextCursor! : undefined;\r\n' +

    '        loadedQuery.current = filterKey;\r\n' +
    "        writeSessionCache(parametersCacheKey(filters.q ?? '', rosterKey, day), refreshed);\r\n" +

    '        .catch(async (cause) => {\r\n' +
    '          if (!controller.signal.aborted && version === generation.current) {\r\n' +
    '            if (isRosterChanged(cause) && (await recoverRoster())) return;\r\n' +
    '            setError(cause.message);\r\n' +
    '          }\r\n' +
    '        })\r\n' +
    '        .finally(() => {\r\n' +
    '          if (version === generation.current && !controller.signal.aborted) {\r\n' +
    '            setLoading(false);\r\n' +
    '            setSummaryLoading(false);\r\n' +
    '          }\r\n' +
    '        });\r\n' +
    '    };\r\n' +
    '    const typing = previousQuery.current !== query;\r\n' +
    '    previousQuery.current = query;\r\n' +
    '    const timer = window.setTimeout(load, typing ? 250 : 0);\r\n' +
    '    return () => {\r\n' +
    '      window.clearTimeout(timer);\r\n' +
    '      controller.abort();\r\n' +
    '      moreController.current?.abort();\r\n' +
    '      generation.current = version + 1;\r\n' +
    '    };\r\n' +
    '  }, [\r\n' +
    '    filters,\r\n' +
    '    revision,\r\n' +
    '    rosterKey,\r\n' +
